## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner on this Mac. For example, I can fix a failing test in a repo, or keep an eye on something and tell you when it changes.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 도와드릴게요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 고치거나, 웹에서 정보를 찾아 정리하거나, 일정·메일 같은 계정 정보를 확인하는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 오늘 무엇부터 해볼까요?
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 봐요. 📝
--- step 2: user says: OK

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたことを確かめながら、順番どおり確実に届ける」通信方式で、UDPは「確認や順序保証をせず、速さを優先して送りっぱなしにする」通信方式です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local, antes de compartirla o abrir un PR, y así tener un historial lineal. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya es pública o que otros usan, porque reescribir su historial con rebase les causaría problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的，保证数据有序送达、丢了会重传；UDP 无连接、不保证可靠，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when you want a clean, linear history for your own branch that hasn't been shared yet, such as tidying up before opening a PR. Merge when the branch is shared or already public, since rebasing rewrites commits and breaks others' copies.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data, so if the system crashes midway, it can replay the log to restore a consistent state. This lets the database write quickly and safely, because it only has to append to a sequential log rather than update scattered data pages on every commit.
```

  .k-r … d('empty');
  const cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  const sub = document.getElementById('sub');

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
  }

  function render(trains, updated) {
    const items = Array.isArray(trains) ? trains : [];
    if (!items.length) {
      list.innerHTML = '';
      empty.hidden = false;
      cnt.textContent = '还没记';
      cnt.classList.add('k-none');
      ducter.summary('还没记');
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    cnt.textContent = items.length + ' 班';
    cnt.classList.remove('k-none');
    list.innerHTML = items.map(t =>
      '<div class="k-row"><div><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.no) + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(t.seats || '') + '</div></div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div></div>'
    ).join('');
    sub.textContent = updated ? '更新于 ' + updated : '每天早上看一次';
    ducter.summary(items.length + ' 班 600 元以下');
  }

  function load() {
    Promise.all([ducter.get('trains'), ducter.get('updated')])
      .then(([t, u]) => render(t, u));
  }

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>

(function () {
  var KEY = 'beijing-trip';
  var WEEK = ['日', '一', '二', '三', '四', '五', '六'];
  var state = { days: [{ date: '2026-11-24', items: [] }] };
  var pending = null;

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });

  function money(n) { return '¥' + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100).toFixed(2); }
  function parse(iso) { return new Date(iso + 'T00:00:00'); }
  function label(iso) {
    var d = parse(iso);
    return (d.getMonth() + 1) + '月' + d.getDate() + '日 周' + WEEK[d.getDay()];
  }
  function dayTotal(d) {
    return d.items.reduce(function (s, it) { return s + it.amount; }, 0);
  }
  function grandTotal() {
    return state.days.reduce(function (s, d) { return s + dayTotal(d); }, 0);
  }
  function countItems() { … e.days[state.days.length - 1].date;
      var d = parse(last);
      d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
      var iso = d.getFullYear() + '-' + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
      state.days.push({ date: iso, items: [] });
      pending = null;
      save();
    } else if (act === 'add') {
      var i = Number(el.getAttribute('data-day'));
      var name = document.getElementById('n' + i).value.trim();
      var amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('a' + i).value);
      if (!name || !(amt > 0)) return;
      state.days[i].items.push({ name: name, amount: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100 });
      pending = null;
      save();
    } else if (act === 'rm') {
      var di = Number(el.getAttribute('data-day'));
      var ji = Number(el.getAttribute('data-item'));
      if (pending && pending.day === di && pending.item === ji) {
        state.days[di].items.splice(ji, 1);
        pending = null;
        save();
      } else {
        pending = { day: di, item: ji };
        render();
      }
    }
  });

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(function (v) {
    if (v && v.days) state = v;
    render();
  });
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

<main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Today</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="today">0 / 3</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="best">Tap a day to tick it off</div>
  </div>
  <section id="list"></section>
</main>
<script>
const HABITS = ducter.get("habits") || [];

const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const iso = d => `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
const dayAgo = n => { const d = new Date(); d.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0); d.setDate(d.getDate() - n); return iso(d); };

let checks = {};

function streak( … ntById('list').innerHTML = HABITS.map(h => {
    const set = checks[h.id] || {};
    let strip = '';
    for (let i = 13; i >= 0; i--) {
      const d = dayAgo(i);
      strip += `<button class="hb-day${i === 0 ? ' hb-today' : ''}" aria-pressed="${!!set[d]}" data-h="${h.id}" data-d="${d}" title="${d}">${Number(d.slice(8))}</button>`;
    }
    return `<div class="k-card hb-card">
      <div><b>${h.name}</b><div class="k-row-sub">${h.sub}</div></div>
      <div class="hb-stats">
        <div class="hb-stat"><b>${streak(set)}</b><span>day streak</span></div>
        <div class="hb-stat"><b>${best(set)}</b><span>best</span></div>
      </div>
      <div class="hb-strip">${strip}</div>
    </div>`;
  }).join('');
}

document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('.hb-day');
  if (!b) return;
  const h = b.dataset.h, d = b.dataset.d;
  checks[h] = checks[h] || {};
  if (checks[h][d]) delete checks[h][d]; else checks[h][d] = true;
  ducter.set('checks', checks);
  render();
});

async function load() {
  checks = (await ducter.get('checks')) || {};
  render();
}

if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>

  <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>

function today(){const d=new Date();return d.getFullYear()+"-"+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")+"-"+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  const t=document.getElementById("total");
  if(xs.length){t.textContent="¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN");t.classList.remove("k-none");}
  else{t.textContent="还没记";t.classList.add("k-none");}
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"出差花的每一笔都在这里";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent="¥"+Number(x.yuan).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(sub);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length? xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
}
document.getElementById("day").value=today();
document.getElementById("add").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||today();
  if(!what||!(yuan>0))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

  $('edit').hidden = false;
  $('edit').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'nearest'});
});

$('del').addEventListener('click', () => {

});
$('close').addEventListener('click', () => { editId = null; $('edit').hidden = true; });

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf as "PRs waiting on others", with the three waiting PRs and the draft. Tick each one off as it lands and the count at the top drops.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1415 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">–</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">Your PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<script>
var ITEMS=ducter.get("items") || [];
var done={};
function draw(){
  document.getElementById('list').innerHTML = ITEMS.map(function(i){
    var on = !!done[i.id];
    return '<button class="k-row" data-id="'+i.id+'" aria-pressed="'+on+'" style="width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:0;color:inherit;font:inherit;cursor:pointer;opacity:'+(on?0.45:1)+'">'
      + '<span class="k-row-main">'+(on?'☑ ':'☐ ')+i.t+'</span>'
      + '<span class="k-row-sub">'+i.ref+' · '+i.s+'</span></button>';
  }).join('');
  var left = ITEMS.filter(function(i){return !done[i.id];}).length;
  document.getElementById('left').textContent = left;
  if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(left+' still open');
}
function load(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function(v){ done = v || {}; draw(); });
}
document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function(e){
  var b = e.target.closest('button[data-id]'); if(!b) return;
  var id = b.getAttribute('data-id');
  done[id] = !done[id];
  ducter.set('done', done);
  draw();
});
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"d412","ref":"wey-gu/ducter #412","t":"Compaction cut","s":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review since Mon"},{"id":"m10287","ref":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","t":"Retry on 502","s":"CI is failing"},{"id":"h1592","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","t":"Flaky compaction test","s":"Waiting on Alice since Tue"},{"id":"d418","ref":"wey-gu/ducter #418","t":"Apps bar","s":"Draft"}]}
